- **skills-sync**: Detects and resolves drift between .claude/skills/&lt;name&gt;/SKILL.md and .github/skills/&lt;name&gt;/SKILL.md. Also validates that every skill has a matching command (.claude/commands/&lt;name&gt;.md) and prompt (.github/prompts/&lt;name&gt;.prompt.md). Invoked exclusively by the agent-manager agent.
